Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a atmospheres algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 686. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 980/4.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^980(S, F) is trivial. QED.
